Cells as items (what each does: weapons/armor.txt)  (items/cells.txt)
Generated by python -m tools.atlas areas. Marks: Class_C? / table[Row]? / asset? = not in the atlas; ITEM{catalog} = an item id and the catalog that has it; ROW<behemoths> = a behemoth sheet exists; Name~ = looks only (flare colour variants, atmospheres, animations, UI), not dumped.
